Written by Claude (Opus 5.5) on 2026-11-13, New York time; not reviewed by a human.  Every
reading is attributed to the transcription that has it; nothing here is about the Leningrad
Codex itself.  Hebrew is copied from the data by script and shown in MAM-normal mark order.

== 1. Method
Every verse's morphhb words are aligned with MAM's atoms by letters (difflib on whole
atoms' letters, final forms distinct), the ketiv side (plain and x-ketiv words) with MAM's ketiv
side and the qere side (plain and qere words) with MAM's qere side.  A pair is a morphhb word and
a MAM atom in an equal block (so with the same letters) whose form keys differ; the form key is
the Israel Bible review's key (mhb_common.skey) with a trailing maqaf dropped.  Pairs are deduped
by (morphhb id, MAM's form key).  Every morphhb word is indexed by its form key, with counts of
three analyses: the lemma's main number (its digits, or a bare prefix letter), the full morph, and
the two together.  For each pair and each analysis: own = the other occurrences of morphhb's form
(this word left out), MAM = the occurrences of MAM's form in morphhb.  The analysis "reads MAM's
form" when own has at least OWN_MIN_N occurrences, the analysis's share among them is at most
OWN_MAX_SHARE, MAM's form has at least MAM_MIN_N occurrences and the analysis's share among them is
at least MAM_MIN_SHARE.  A pair is flagged when any of its three analyses reads MAM's form.

A pair whose own form has fewer than OWN_MIN_N other occurrences is inconclusive.  Two back-off
tests, with the same thresholds, replace own's evidence for such a pair: (A) the occurrences in
morphhb of every other form with the same letters (neither morphhb's form nor MAM's); (B) the
morphhb words that are aligned one to one with a MAM atom whose form is morphhb's form here, so
that MAM's text vouches for the form being a real one and morphhb's analyses there show how it is
read.  A rare pair is flagged by a back-off test when one of its analyses reads MAM's form by it.

Supplement: one-to-one pairs whose letters differ (a 1x1 non-equal block of the letters
alignment) get the same tests.

Thresholds: OWN_MIN_N = 5, OWN_MAX_SHARE = 10%, MAM_MIN_N = 1, MAM_MIN_SHARE = 50%.
The lemma's main number ignores morphhb's augment letter ("1004 b" counts as 1004), so that an
inconsistent augment cannot make a flag.

Claude's classes for the candidates read:
  correction a correction: WLC's form, as written, is a regular spelling of another word or of another inflection, and the lemma or the morph analyses MAM's reading instead
  anomaly    an anomaly read as the intended word: WLC's form is no regular word or form in its place (often under a WLC note), and the analysis is that of the word both transcriptions mean, which MAM's form spells regularly; Part 1's class c
  both fit   the forms differ in pointing that neither the lemma nor the morph encodes; the analysis fits both (Part 1's class c)
  defensible a homograph, or an analysis that WLC's form admits as written
  ketiv      a ketiv: the difference is in how a ketiv is written or pointed, or in how morphhb analyses a ketiv
  reads WLC  the analysis reads WLC's form where MAM's form would be analysed otherwise (like Judges 19:4)
  slip       an inconsistency of morphhb's own: the lemma or morph fits neither WLC's form nor MAM's, and the two agree
  noise      flagged, but the analysis fits both forms: the flag comes from inconsistent labels in morphhb or from a coincidence of distributions
  agrees     the chanted word agrees with MAM's by the review key; no correction is possible there
